This site is protected by reCAPTCHA and the Google Privacy Policy and Terms of Service apply. FAQ Who is eligible to buy OUSG? Organizations or individuals in eligible countries - including the US - who have onboarded to our Qualified-Access Funds can invest in, redeem, or transfer, or receive OUSG. See all the ways you can qualify here . How is OUSG backed? The significant majority of this portfolio is currently in the BlackRock USD Institutional Digital Liquidity Fund (BUIDL), with the remainder in BlackRock&#x27;s FedFund (TFDXX), bank deposits, and USDC for liquidity purposes. The portfolio may, in the future, include other US Treasury funds and/or direct investments in US Treasuries. How do I invest? To invest, you must first onboard to our Qualified-Access Funds. Once you complete onboarding, you can invest either USD via wire transfer or via any supported stablecoin. What is the minimum I can invest in OUSG? For instant transactions, the minimum amount is $ 5K for both investments and redemptions . For non-instant transactions, our minimum investment and redemption amounts are $100K and $50K, respectively.
